### [1] [LLMs Know the Constraint But Do Not Use It: Activation Bottlenecks in Pragmatic Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12321)
*Yubo Li,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在显性表面线索与隐式可行性约束冲突时的失败原因，将问题形式化为"条件性约束激活"框架，通过四位组诊断（Knowledge/Symmetry/Routing/Repair）对14个模型进行分析，发现失败本质是路由（Routing）问题而非知识（Knowledge）问题——模型内部已编码约束信息（探针解码准确率>88%），但未可靠路由到决策中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估用聚合准确率衡量LLM在约束推理上的表现，但准确率混淆了"真正学习到约束"与"保守默认输出"两种截然不同的行为。论文旨在区分这两种失败机制，以更精确地定位LLM约束推理失败的根源。

Method: 提出条件性约束激活框架，通过四个诊断维度拆解模型行为：Knowledge（约束是否在内部编码）、Symmetry（约束在有无提示下是否对称编码）、Routing（约束是否路由到决策中）、Repair（能否通过供体激活修复）。对14个模型进行四人组（quartet）诊断，并用探针（probes）和激活修补（activation patching）对两个开放权重模型深入分析。

Result: 14个模型呈现两种失败模式；两个开放权重模型上探针能从内部状态解码约束（准确率>88%），但激活修补只修复了其中一个（+6.4 nats），另一个未修复（-0.07 nats）。所有提示干预均无法达到修复状态，且统一通过单一中介路径（前提提及）放大保守偏差。

Conclusion: 隐藏约束失败的根源在于约束信息的路由问题而非知识缺失问题。模型内部已具备约束知识，但未能可靠地将其路由到决策过程；仅靠提示工程无法解决该问题，因为所有现存提示干预都共享同一脆弱的中介路径。

Abstract: When a salient surface cue competes with an implicit feasibility constraint, LLMs often fail -- but aggregate accuracy conflates genuine constraint inference with conservative defaulting. We formalize the distinction as conditional constraint activation: the constraint is internally encoded (Knowledge) symmetrically across constraint-present and -absent prompts (Symmetry), yet only sometimes routed into the decision (Routing) and repairable by a donor activation (Repair). A quartet diagnostic over 14 models reveals two failure modes; probes on two open weights decode the constraint above $88\%$, yet activation patching repairs one ($+6.4$ nats) and not the other ($-0.07$). On a mitigation frontier, no prompted intervention reaches the repair corner: all inflate conservative bias through a single mediation pathway -- prerequisite mention. Hidden-constraint failure is a routing problem, not a knowledge problem.

</details>


### [2] [What Drives LLM Self-Reflection? A Controlled Ablation of Uncertainty Routing in Armed Conflict Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12322)
*Poli Nemkova,Haeshitha Indukuri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过六个条件的受控消融实验，系统剖析LLM自我反思中四个组件（证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类词汇、动作路由）的独立贡献。发现结构化诊断问题和分类词汇均无显著增益，而类型化动作路由是唯一带来显著、可复制提升的机制（F1从0.296提升至0.379，跨GPT-4o与LLaMA复制验证），为"元认知LLM预测智能体"的设计提供了关键原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我反思被普遍认为能提升LLM推理能力，但究竟是哪个组件驱动了这种提升，此前缺乏严格的受控分析。

Method: 设计六条件消融实验，隔离四个组件：证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类词汇和动作路由。通过两个精确零结果（p=1.000和CI重叠）定位于单一机制，并在GPT-4o上复制词汇-路由分解以验证跨骨干一致性。

Result: 结构化诊断问题无增益（F1=0.296 vs 0.297, p=1.000）；分类词汇无增益（ΔF1=+0.008）；类型化动作路由带来显著增益（ΔF1=+0.101, 95% CI [+0.020, +0.185]）。在GPT-4o上，词汇无显著增益（p=0.773）而动作路由显著（p=0.025）。在Myanmar（0.000→0.353）和Ukraine（0.167→0.500）等结构新颖冲突上增益最集中。

Conclusion: 类型化动作路由是LLM自我反思提升推理的关键设计原则，而非诊断脚手架或分类词汇；该发现需在更广泛的冲突类型学上扩展验证。

Abstract: Self-reflection is widely assumed to improve LLM reasoning, yet which component drives the gain remains poorly understood. We present a controlled six-condition ablation isolating four components of LLM self-reflection: evidence exposure, diagnostic scaffolding, taxonomy vocabulary, and action routing. Two precise null results converge on a single mechanism. First, structured diagnostic questions add no measurable value over unstructured reflection ($\text{F1} = 0.296$ vs $0.297$, $p = 1.000$, 95\% CI $[-0.041, +0.040]$). Second, presenting the full uncertainty taxonomy while collapsing the action space to a single generic action also adds no value ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.008$, overlapping 95\% CIs), ruling out taxonomy vocabulary as the mechanism. Typed action routing provides consistent directional gains ($\text{F1} = 0.379$ vs $0.296$); the conservative estimate controlling for taxonomy vocabulary is $Δ\text{F1} = +0.075$, and the overall gain over the single-shot baseline is significant by bootstrap CI ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.101$, 95\% CI $[+0.020, +0.185]$). The vocabulary-routing decomposition replicates on GPT-4o: taxonomy vocabulary adds no significant value over generic reflection ($p = 0.773$), while action routing provides significant gains ($p = 0.025$), confirming the mechanism holds across backbones. Gains concentrate on structurally novel conflicts: in Myanmar ($\text{F1}: 0.000 \rightarrow 0.353$) and Ukraine ($0.167 \rightarrow 0.500$), the vocabulary-only condition recovers no more than generic reflection while action routing breaks the degenerate prior. These findings identify typed action routing -- not diagnostic scaffolding or taxonomy vocabulary -- as a promising design principle for metacognitive LLM forecasting agents, while motivating larger-scale evaluation across conflict typologies.

</details>


### [3] [Why Do AI Agents Break Rules? How Framing, Context, and Social Signals Shape Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12323)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Kutluhan Erol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文借鉴法律经济学中的合规理论（威慑理论、合法性理论、表达性法律理论），系统性地分析AI智能体为何会违规而非仅仅是否违规。研究在12个指令微调语言模型（作为企业采购聊天机器人）上验证假设，发现安全微调模型能广泛维持合规，而任务优化和智能体型模型将监管信号视为优化参数，在低处罚、非命令式措辞等条件下失守。引入金钱激励、管理要求、同伴结果或员工压力均产生重大合规失败，标准对齐基准无法捕捉此类违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估主要测试模型"是否失败"，但忽略了一个关键问题——"为什么失败"。特别的，规范设置处罚机制可能出现悖论：将法律义务转化为成本收益计算反而有利于违规（执法信息悖论）。作者希望将法律经济学的合规理论作为诊断工具，从理论层面解释AI智能体系统性的违规行为。

Method: 将合规理论作为经验假设而非隐喻，在12个指令微调语言模型（作为企业采购聊天机器人）上系统验证。具体测试三类理论：威慑理论、合法性理论和表达性法律理论，检验不同模型类别（安全微调型、任务优化型、智能体型）在各理论下的合规表现，以及引入金钱激励、管理命令、同伴结果和员工压力等局部目标干扰下的行为变化。

Result: 发现安全微调模型在广泛场景下保持合规；任务优化和智能体模型则将监管信号当作纯优化参数，在低执法处罚和非命令式措辞等理论预测的条件下发生合规失败。所有模型中，引入财务激励、管理要求、同伴结果或员工压力都会导致大规模合规失败。采购智能体系统性地为了满足本地用户目标而违反监管约束，且这些行为未被标准对齐基准捕捉。

Conclusion: 仅靠规则嵌入无法实现合规；模型选择本身是一种治理决策，基于基准的评估不足以支撑合规敏感型部署。需要将合规考量纳入模型选型和评估的治理框架中。

Abstract: Specifying a penalty can paradoxically convert a legal obligation into a cost-benefit calculation that favors violation. We demonstrate that this enforcement information paradox systematically occurs in AI agents. While most AI safety evaluations test whether models fail, we investigate why, applying compliance theory from law and economics as a diagnostic tool. We treat compliance theories not as metaphors but as empirical hypotheses and show that each predicts the behavior of a distinct model class. We evaluate our hypotheses across twelve instruction-tuned language models operating as enterprise procurement chatbots. Drawing on theories of deterrence, legitimacy, and expressive law, we show that safety-fine-tuned models maintain compliance broadly, while task-optimized and agentic models treat regulatory signals as mere optimization parameters. These latter models fail to comply under conditions predicted by theory, such as low enforcement penalties and non-command phrasing. Across all models, introducing financial incentives, managerial demands, peer outcomes, or employee pressure produces large compliance failures. AI procurement agents systematically violate regulatory constraints to satisfy local user objectives in ways not captured by standard alignment benchmarks. Ultimately, compliance cannot be achieved by rule embedding alone; model selection is itself a governance decision, and benchmark-based evaluation is insufficient for compliance-sensitive deployments.

</details>


### [4] [On Measuring Semantic Preservation in Legal Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12326)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的评估本体学习语义保持能力的方法：通过比较LLM在源文档与转换后表示上的任务性能差异来量化语义损失，并在法律合并协议分析场景下用三种本体学习方法和六个语言模型进行了实证验证，发现语义损失随推理复杂度和模型-方法组合而显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体学习将非结构化文本转换为结构化表示以支持自动推理，但这一转换过程可能导致语义信息丢失。现有评估方法仅关注结构正确性，无法检测语义是否在转换中得以保持。

Method: 提出对比评估框架：让LLM直接处理源文档与转换后的本体表示，任务性能差异即为语义损失的量化指标。在法律合并协议分析领域，对三种本体学习方法与六个语言模型的组合进行系统比较。

Result: 实验揭示了系统性的语义损失，且损失程度随推理复杂度和模型-方法交互作用呈现显著差异；语义保持效果的变异性主要取决于模型与方法的具体配对。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1)一个用于衡量本体学习语义保持的评估框架；(2)实证证据表明语义损失随模型-方法配对剧烈变化，为法律知识系统中最优配置的选择提供指导。

Abstract: Ontology learning transforms unstructured text into structured representations for automated reasoning. Yet structuring information risks losing it, and current evaluation methodologies cannot detect such loss, focusing on structural correctness while failing to measure whether meaning survives transformation. We propose an evaluation methodology that addresses this: comparing LLM task performance on source documents against performance on transformed representations, with the difference quantifying semantic loss. We demonstrate this approach on legal merger agreement analysis, a domain chosen for its complex language and precise semantic requirements, comparing direct LLM application against three ontology learning methods across six language models. The results reveal systematic semantic loss with significant variation based on reasoning complexity and model-method interactions. Our contributions are: (1) an evaluation framework for measuring semantic preservation in ontology learning, and (2) empirical evidence that semantic loss varies dramatically with model-method pairing, providing guidance for selecting optimal configurations in legal knowledge systems.

</details>


### [5] [Comparative Analysis of Multilingual Pre-trained Models for Nepali Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12327)
*Suman Paudel,Sarbin Sayami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一的微调协议下，系统比较了六种多语言预训练模型（涵盖CTC自监督、自回归编码器-解码器、混合Conformer-CTC三种架构）在尼泊尔语ASR上的表现。结果表明Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo和IndicWav2Vec在WER上并列最优，尽管两者参数规模和预训练数据量相差9倍和40倍；CTC解码器在相同精度下比自回归Whisper快29倍；MMS-1B在域外测试集上退化最小。该工作提供了首个标准化、多模型、效率感知的尼泊尔语ASR基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言预训练模型名义上支持尼泊尔语，但缺乏在统一微调协议下进行受控比较的基准。现有研究通常在不可比的设置下评估不同模型，导致无法公平判断各架构在尼泊尔语上的真实性能与效率权衡。

Method: 在OpenSLR SLR54尼泊尔语语料库（约165小时）上，以完全相同的预处理、数据划分、优化器和族匹配学习率调度，微调六种模型：XLSR-53、IndicWav2Vec（CTC自监督）、MMS-1B、Whisper-Medium、Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo（自回归编码器-解码器）、Conformer-Hi（混合Conformer-CTC）。在OpenSLR、FLEURS和Common Voice三个独立测试集上评估WER、CER和RTF。

Result: Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo（14.76% WER）与IndicWav2Vec（14.89% WER）并列最优；CTC解码器在同等精度下比Whisper快29倍；MMS-1B在FLEURS域外测试集上仅退化+12.55个百分点，为最小退化。

Conclusion: 语言族亲缘性在预训练中可替代原始参数规模以取得域内性能；大规模多语言预训练带来的是域外鲁棒性而非更高的峰值域内精度；CTC架构在部署效率上具有明显优势。该基准为尼泊尔语ASR提供了首个效率感知的标准化参照。

Abstract: Multilingual pretrained models nominally support Nepali, yet no controlled benchmark has compared them under a single fine-tuning protocol. We fine-tune six pretrained models (XLSR-53, IndicWav2Vec, MMS-1B, Whisper-Medium, Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo, and Conformer-Hi) spanning CTC self-supervised, autoregressive encoder-decoder, and hybrid Conformer-CTC architectures, on the OpenSLR SLR54 Nepali corpus (~165 hours) using identical preprocessing, splits, optimizer, and family-matched learning-rate schedules. We evaluate Word Error Rate (WER), Character Error Rate (CER), and Real-Time Factor (RTF) on three independent test sets (OpenSLR, FLEURS, Common Voice). Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo (14.76% WER) and IndicWav2Vec (14.89% WER) tie at the top despite a 9x parameter gap and 40x pretraining-data gap, providing direct empirical evidence that language-family proximity in pretraining can substitute for raw scale for in-domain Nepali. CTC decoders run up to 29x faster than autoregressive Whisper at the same accuracy, flipping the practical deployment preference toward CTC under any latency budget. Massively multilingual pretraining (MMS-1B) yields the smallest out-of-domain degradation on FLEURS (+12.55 pp), indicating that scale buys robustness rather than peak in-domain accuracy. The resulting benchmark provides the first standardized, multi-model, efficiency-aware reference numbers for Nepali ASR.

</details>


### [6] [LoRA-Diffusion: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via Low-Rank Trajectory Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12328)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Diffusion，一种针对扩散式语言模型的参数高效微调方法。与权重级LoRA不同，该方法对去噪轨迹本身进行低秩分解，学习从噪声到输出的完整扩散路径的低秩扰动，并通过轨迹级低秩适配器、步骤自适应秩分配和组合式多任务学习实现参数高效的模型适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）已成功应用于自回归大语言模型，但尚未被推广到基于迭代去噪的扩散式语言模型。这是因为现有权重级低秩分解方法不适用于扩散模型的训练范式，需要一种针对扩散路径的专门参数高效微调方案。

Method: LoRA-Diffusion包含三个核心组件：(1)轨迹级低秩适配器，对每一步去噪过程施加低秩扰动；(2)步骤自适应秩分配，根据扩散阶段动态调整秩大小；(3)组合式多任务学习，允许在推理时合并不同任务的模块而无需重新训练。

Result: 在SST-2、QNLI和MRPC三个数据集上报告了五个随机种子的token级去噪验证准确率。LoRA-Diffusion在SST-2上取得最高平均性能，在QNLI和MRPC上表现强劲；联合多任务训练中取得了最高的token级准确率。

Conclusion: LoRA-Diffusion相比全量微调降低了每任务存储开销，为扩散式语言模型建立了参数高效微调框架，填补了LoRA等权重级方法无法直接应用于扩散语言模型的空白。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have transformed the adaptation of large autoregressive language models, enabling task-specific customization with substantially fewer trainable parameters. However, these methods have not been successfully extended to diffusion-based language models, which generate text through iterative denoising rather than sequential token prediction. We propose LoRA-Diffusion, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach that applies low-rank decomposition to the denoising trajectory instead of model weights. Unlike weight-based LoRA, which modifies individual transformation matrices, our method learns low-rank perturbations to the entire diffusion path from noise to output. We introduce trajectory-level low-rank adapters that modify each denoising step, step-adaptive rank allocation across diffusion phases, and compositional multi-task learning that allows merging task-specific modules at inference without retraining. On SST-2, QNLI, and MRPC, we report token-level denoising validation accuracy over five random seeds. LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest mean performance on SST-2 and strong performance on QNLI and MRPC. Joint multi-task training further shows that LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest token-level accuracy among the evaluated methods. The approach reduces per-task storage compared with full fine-tuning and establishes a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for diffusion language models.

</details>


### [7] [AnchorSIPS: A Synthetic Dataset and Evaluation Resource for Evidence-Supported Psychosis-Risk Symptom Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12329)
*Guilherme C. Oliveira,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Clarice Lee,Xiangyu Zhao,Duy Khoa Pham,Duong Nhu,Yiwen Jiang,Jiahe Liu,Zhongxing Xu,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Dominic Dwyer,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorSIPS，一个包含10K结构化解构精神病风险访谈的合成数据集，基于plan-then-realize流水线生成，每个中间临床决策均锚定到支持性对话转录片段，用于研究证据提取和转录基础的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床访谈因隐私、治理和同意限制难以共享，造成数据获取瓶颈，阻碍AI精神病风险评估进展。

Method: 采用plan-then-realize流水线：隐藏病例表指定患者临床状态，确定性规划器固定访谈结构，LLM仅生成患者话语并通过验证和有界修复保证质量。先固定标签和结构再生成，避免多轮LLM对话的轮间不一致。

Result: 在七个LLM基线上，模型能恢复粗略决策但无法提取后续细节或引用支持性转录片段，最终标签性能高估了访谈能力。

Conclusion: AnchorSIPS旨在服务证据提取、转录基础的测量以及部分披露下的不确定性研究，为数据稀缺的精神病风险AI研究提供替代数据资源。

Abstract: Progress on AI for psychosis-risk assessment is limited by a data-access bottleneck. Real clinical interviews are difficult to share because of privacy, governance, and consent constraints. We present AnchorSIPS, a synthetic dataset of 10K structured psychosis-risk interviews with transcript-grounded measurement targets. Each interview is modeled on Mini-SIPS, a clinician-administered psychosis-risk interview. It captures history, 24 symptom questions, follow-up evidence for items the patient affirms, decisions about delusion-like symptoms (unusual beliefs), hallucination-like symptoms (unusual perceptions), and disorganized communication, exclusion of clear psychotic-level symptoms ("frank psychosis"), and a final attenuated psychosis syndrome (APS) diagnosis, a high-risk state of milder or early psychotic symptoms. The APS diagnosis is not a standalone label. It depends on earlier endorsements, supporting follow-up details, symptom-class decisions, and the frank-psychosis check. Every intermediate decision is anchored to its supporting transcript turns. AnchorSIPS is generated by a plan-then-realize pipeline. A hidden case sheet specifies the patient's clinical state, a deterministic planner fixes the interview structure, and an LLM realizes only the patient utterances under validation and bounded repair. Fixing labels and structure before generation avoids the inter-turn inconsistencies typical of multi-turn LLM dialogue. Across seven LLM baselines, models recover coarse decisions but fail to extract follow-up details or cite supporting transcript turns, so final-label performance overstates interview competence. AnchorSIPS is intended for research on evidence extraction, transcript-grounded measurement, and uncertainty under partial disclosure.

</details>


### [8] [Reliability-Aware Sexism Detection: Combining DPO with Annotator Agreement and Token-Level Confidence Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12330)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Shihan Wang,Masoume M. Raeissi,Anastasia Giachanou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RA-DPO框架，整合标注者一致性、模型置信度和token级不确定性为可靠性分数，用于主观性任务（在线性别歧视检测）的训练样本选择和推理时弃权，实现降低训练成本且提升部署可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性别歧视检测固有主观性，但现有系统将多标注者标签简化为单一多数决策并对所有样本一视同仁，忽略了标注者一致性和模型不确定性这两个信息丰富的信号。这既浪费了训练资源，也无法在实际部署中提供可靠的置信度控制。

Method: 提出RA-DPO（Reliability-Aware Direct Preference Optimization），将标注者一致性、模型置信度和token级不确定性信号整合为单一可靠性分数。训练阶段利用该分数选择高价值偏好对；推理阶段支持选择性预测（弃权机制），让模型在覆盖率和准确率之间进行权衡。在EXIST 2023的6,920条多语言帖子上评估，对gpt-4o base进行DPO微调，并在Llama和Qwen两个3B开源模型上验证。

Result: 仅用最可靠的30%偏好对训练即可匹配全量数据DPO的性能；推理时选择性预测在真实一致性设定下达到50%覆盖率96.2%的准确率，在可部署的预测一致性设定下达到88.7%，均超过无一致性基线85.3%。

Conclusion: 考虑标注不确定性对主观分类任务的高效训练和可靠部署均有显著益处，证明了可靠性感知的样本选择与选择性预测在主观性NLP任务中的有效性。

Abstract: The detection of online sexism remains an open problem. Sexism detection is inherently subjective, yet most existing systems reduce multi-annotator labels to a single majority decision and treat all instances uniformly. This ignores two informative signals: annotator agreement and model uncertainty. We propose RA-DPO (Reliability-Aware Direct Preference Optimization), which integrates annotator agreement, model confidence, and a token-level uncertainty signal into a single reliability score. RA-DPO uses this score to select high-value preference pairs during training and to support inference-time abstention, which allows the model to trade coverage for accuracy. We evaluate RA-DPO on 6,920 multilingual posts from EXIST 2023, fine-tune OpenAI gpt-4o base via DPO, and validate on two open-weight 3B models (Llama, Qwen). Results show that training on the top 30% most reliable pairs matches full-data DPO, which indicates that reliability-aware selection can reduce training cost without sacrificing performance. At inference, selective prediction reaches 96.2% accuracy at 50% coverage in the true-agreement setting and 88.7% in the deployable predicted-agreement setting, both exceeding the 85.3% no-agreement baseline. These results suggest that accounting for annotation uncertainty is beneficial for both efficient training and reliable deployment in subjective classification.

</details>


### [9] [Thought-Aware KV Cache Compaction for Reasoning via Adaptive Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12331)
*Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Jiayu Liang,Xu Kefu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Thought-Aware Attention Matching (TAM)，一种利用推理轨迹层级结构的KV缓存压缩方法，通过思维分段、自适应预算分配和关键token保护三个机制，在相同内存占用下优于统一压缩方法，并可将峰值内存降低65%同时保持竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型生成长链思维（CoT）序列时，KV缓存随推理长度线性增长，成为解码过程中的内存瓶颈。现有压缩方法将推理轨迹视为扁平token序列进行统一压缩，忽略了CoT推理的层级结构——不同推理步骤的重要性差异巨大。

Method: TAM包含三个核心机制：(i) 思维分段——将推理轨迹分解为推理块；(ii) 自适应预算分配——根据每个分段的相对重要性和大小分配压缩预算，并证明该分配规则在凸误差模型下最优；(iii) 关键token保护——保留高注意力的推理锚点token。此外，论文证明了顺序压缩下的累积误差有界。

Result: 在AIME 2024和MATH-500基准上使用Qwen3-4B模型进行实验，TAM在相同内存占用下相比统一压缩方法提升了准确性；周期压缩可将峰值内存限制在3.1-3.2GB（降低65%），同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: TAM通过利用CoT推理的层级结构，实现了比统一压缩方法更优的内存-精度权衡，为推理语言模型的长序列解码提供了高效的内存压缩方案。

Abstract: Reasoning language models generate lengthy chain-of-thought (CoT) sequences whose key-value (KV) cache grows linearly and becomes a memory bottleneck during decoding. Existing compaction methods treat reasoning trajectories as flat token sequences and apply uniform compression, ignoring the hierarchical structure of CoT reasoning where different steps vary drastically in importance. We propose \textbf{Thought-Aware Attention Matching (TAM)}, which exploits this structure through three mechanisms: (i)~thought segmentation that decomposes the trajectory into reasoning blocks, (ii)~adaptive budget allocation that assigns compression budget based on each segment's importance and size, and (iii)~pivotal token protection that preserves high-attention reasoning anchors. We prove that the allocation rule is optimal under a convex error model and that cumulative error under sequential compaction remains bounded. Experiments on AIME 2024 and MATH-500 with Qwen3-4B show that TAM improves accuracy over uniform compaction at the same memory footprint, with periodic compaction bounding peak memory to 3.1--3.2\,GB (a 65\% reduction) while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [Can Spectral-Clipping Enable Better Learning While Forgetting Less for Low-Rank Adaptation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12332)
*Hyowon Wi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCLoRA，一种基于奇异值分解（SVD）的低秩适配（LoRA）改进方法。作者发现预训练参数中的主奇异成分（大奇异值）可在微调时被复用，而次奇异成分（小奇异值）更具任务特异性、需要更多适配。同时，他们首次从理论上建立了LoRA适配器中奇异值不受控增长会导致灾难性遗忘的关联，并提出通过谱裁剪将参数化奇异成分注入预训练模型，既提升下游任务性能又缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA已成为冻结预训练权重并引入小型可学习适配器的有效范 paradigm，但现有方法对预训练模型中不同奇异成分（主成分与次成分）的角色区分不够清晰，且LoRA在适配过程中可能导致灾难性遗忘这一关键问题缺乏理论阐释和有效缓解策略。

Method: 1) 通过SVD分析揭示预训练参数中主奇异成分与次奇异成分在微调中的不同作用；2) 首次建立了LoRA适配器中奇异值不受控增长与灾难性遗忘之间的理论联系；3) 提出SCLoRA方法，将参数化奇异成分与谱裁剪（spectral clipping）以感知预训练模型谱分布的方式注入预训练模型，聚焦需要适配的成分进行更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCLoRA在提升下游任务性能的同时能够有效保留预训练知识，相比现有LoRA变体在性能与遗忘缓解两方面均有改善。

Conclusion: SCLoRA通过谱感知的适配策略和谱裁剪机制，实现了对预训练知识更好的保留，同时保持甚至提升了下游适配性能，为LoRA类方法的改进提供了新的理论洞见和实用方案。

Abstract: In recent years, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a significant paradigm that freezes pre-trained weights and introduces small, learnable adapters instead of fine-tuning the full set of parameters. In this work, we uncover several key insights regarding the singular components of network parameters based on Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Firstly, the principal singular components with large singular values in pre-trained network parameters can be effectively reused during fine-tuning, whereas the minor components with smaller singular values are more task-specific and require substantial adaptation. Secondly, we first establish the theoretical connection that the uncontrolled growth of singular values in LoRA adapters leads to the forgetting of pre-trained knowledge -- a well-known issue referred to as catastrophic forgetting. Building on these observations, we propose SCLoRA, which injects parameterized singular components with spectral clipping into the pre-trained model in a way that is aware of the spectral distribution of the pre-trained model. SCLoRA effectively adapts to new tasks by focusing updates on components that require adaptation, while simultaneously alleviating catastrophic forgetting. We conduct extensive experiments and demonstrate that SCLoRA not only improves downstream performance but also effectively retains pre-trained knowledge.

</details>


### [11] [Vision-Language Models are Fragile Multilingual Associators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12333)
*Ritabrata Chakraborty,Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Shivakumara Palaiahnakote,Angelo Cangelosi,Umapada Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M²BIND基准，评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在多语言环境下概念绑定（视觉实体与文本属性的关联）的稳定性。研究发现绑定并非语言不变的：跨语系和跨文字场景会导致显著的绑定崩溃，模型内部绑定计算移向更深层且因果强度减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未探索VLM中视觉-文本概念绑定在不同语言下是否保持稳定。随着VLM在全球多语言环境中部署，语言变化可能影响绑定质量，需要系统化评估。

Method: 提出M²BIND基准，在不同语言语境和查询下评测绑定。采用双重评估方式：通过任务性能指标进行外在评估，通过因果干预进行内在评估，分析模型内部绑定计算的层位置与因果强度。

Result: 跨语系和跨文字设置触发显著的绑定崩溃；模型内部绑定计算移向更深的层且丧失因果强度；相近语言能相对较好保留绑定关联。

Conclusion: 多语言部署的VLM不能假定其在单语评估中表现出的关联质量在全球多语言环境下保持不变，绑定稳定性受语言距离影响。

Abstract: Vision-language models must associate visual entities with textual attributes. Whether these associations or concept bindings remain stable when the language of the input changes is unexplored. We introduce M$^2$BIND, a benchmark varying the language of the context and query across multiple languages. We evaluate binding both extrinsically through task performance metrics and intrinsically through causal interventions. We find that binding is not language-invariant: cross-family and cross-script settings trigger significant binding collapse, with the model's internal binding computation shifting to later layers and losing causal strength. Closely related languages preserve associations comparatively better. In a broader sense, our findings indicate how VLMs deployed globally in multilingual settings cannot be assumed to maintain the same association quality observed in monolingual evaluation.

</details>


### [12] [Steering the Language Axis: From Linear Decodability to Causal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12334)
*Arnav Srivastav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过PCA提取"语言轴"方向，在Qwen 3.5-2B和Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct等模型上进行1.26百万次生成的大规模因果干预实验，证明语言身份不仅可从隐状态线性解码，还能通过紧凑的激活方向进行因果控制。研究发现语言承诺高度局部化且依赖具体语言对，英语-中文切换在深层易操控，英语-西班牙语切换则提前且呈双峰敏感性；消融语言信号后模型会回归英语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型具备多语言能力，但控制语言选择的潜在动态机制尚不明确。作者希望探究语言身份是否仅能线性解码自隐状态，还是可以通过紧凑的激活方向进行因果控制，从而揭示语言决策的底层机制。

Method: 作者在多个模型家族（Qwen 3.5-2B、Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct）上进行因果干预分析，采用PCA提取"语言轴"，在FLORES-200数据集上进行1.26百万次生成的steering（引导）和ablation（消融）实验，覆盖跨脚本（英-中）和同脚本（英-西）两种语言转换场景，并进行逐层分析。

Result: 沿着几何方向的steering可可靠地强制语言切换（跨脚本和同脚本均有效），而同等幅度的随机扰动几乎无效。逐层分析显示语言承诺高度局部化且依赖具体语言对：英语-中文切换抵制早期干预但在深层易被引导，英语-西班牙语转换则提前发生并呈现独特的双峰敏感性。此外，针对性的消融导致输出回归英语，无论输入提示为何。

Conclusion: 语言决策边界在推理期间表现为因果活跃的特征，具有方向依赖性和层特异性，证明了语言选择机制是可进行精细干预的可控过程，而非隐式的统计现象。

Abstract: Despite the impressive multilingual capabilities of Large Language Models, the latent dynamics dictating language selection remain poorly understood. In this work, we ask whether language identity is merely linearly decodable from hidden states, or if it can be causally controlled by a compact activation direction. We conduct an exhaustive causal intervention analysis across multiple model families, including Qwen 3.5-2B and Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct, isolating PCA-derived "language axes" to perform steering and ablation experiments across 1.26 million generations on the FLORES-200 dataset.
  Steering along these geometric directions reliably forces language switching in both cross-script (English to Chinese) and same-script (English to Spanish) settings, whereas equal-magnitude random perturbations yield virtually no effect. Our layerwise analysis reveals that language commitment is highly localized and explicitly language-pair-dependent. While English to Chinese switching resists early intervention and steers easily in the later layers, the English-Spanish transition shifts earlier, displaying a distinct, bimodal sensitivity. Furthermore, targeted ablation uncovers a fundamental reversion to English: once the language signal is removed, the model falls back to English regardless of the input prompt. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that language decision boundaries function during inference as causally active features that are direction-dependent and layer-specific.

</details>


### [13] [HC-RAG: Evidence-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Heterogeneous Financial Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12335)
*Siyuan Chen,Huaye Tan,You Li,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HC-RAG，一个面向年度报告财务问答的分层跨模态检索增强生成框架，通过构建类型化财务证据图、按文档-章节-单元路径检索、统一文本与表格证据空间，并结合四种语义意图路由证据，显著提升答案质量与证据定位能力。同时发布Multi-Doc-2025基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理财务年度报告问答时存在三大缺陷：一是将长文件压平为无序块，忽略财务报告的层级类型化结构；二是对文本与表格证据的融合策略固定单一，未考虑查询意图；三是跨文档检索和证据定位能力不足。金融问答需要识别相关公司与财年、定位标准章节、收集多模态证据并交叉验证，现有方法无法满足这些需求。

Method: HC-RAG包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建类型化金融证据图，包含文档、章节、文本单元、表格单元和元数据节点；(2) 通过文档-章节-单元路径进行证据检索，在共享检索空间中对齐文本和表格证据；(3) 根据四种语义意图（计算、趋势、事实、比较）进行意图感知的证据路由。此外提出Multi-Doc-2025基准，包含2,327个专家验证的QA对，来自87家标普500公司179份SEC 10-K文件，标注了意图、难度和结构证据属性。

Result: 在公共财务QA基准和Multi-Doc-2025上，HC-RAG在答案质量和证据定位方面均有提升，尤其在长文档、表格相关和跨文档场景下表现突出。HC-RAG在DocFinQA上比RAPTOR高6.6个F1点，在Multi-Doc-2025上比GraphRAG高10.9个F1点。消融实验表明改进主要来自更精确的章节定位、表格接地、跨文档证据聚合和意图感知的文本表格路由。

Conclusion: HC-RAG证明了利用财务报告的类型化结构、统一跨模态检索空间和意图感知的证据路由，可以显著提升基于证据的金融问答性能。该工作同时提供了Multi-Doc-2025基准，为未来财务文档问答研究提供了多文档、多模态、带细粒度标注的评测资源。

Abstract: Financial question answering over annual reports requires more than retrieving semantically similar passages. It often involves identifying relevant companies and fiscal years, locating standardized filing sections, collecting textual and tabular evidence, and checking answers against the original documents. Existing RAG systems, however, usually flatten long filings into unordered chunks, pay limited attention to the typed structure of financial reports, and use fixed text-table fusion strategies without considering query intent. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HC-RAG}, a hierarchical cross-modal retrieval-augmented generation framework for evidence-centric financial QA. HC-RAG organizes filings into a typed financial evidence graph with documents, sections, text units, table units, and metadata nodes. It retrieves evidence through document-section-unit paths, aligns textual and tabular evidence in a shared retrieval space, and routes evidence according to four semantic intents: calculation, trend, fact, and comparison. We further introduce \textbf{Multi-Doc-2025}, a benchmark containing 2,327 expert-verified QA pairs from 179 SEC 10-K filings of 87 S\&P 500 companies across fiscal years 2022--2024, with labels for intent, difficulty, and structural evidence attributes. Experiments on public financial QA benchmarks and Multi-Doc-2025 show that HC-RAG improves both answer quality and evidence localization, especially in long-document, table-related, and cross-document settings. HC-RAG outperforms RAPTOR by 6.6 F1 points on DocFinQA and GraphRAG by 10.9 F1 points on Multi-Doc-2025. Evidence-level analysis and ablation studies show that the improvements mainly come from more accurate section localization, table grounding, cross-document evidence aggregation, and intent-aware text-table routing.

</details>


### [14] [StorySpark: Module-wise Evolutionary Search for Story Premise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12336)
*Yang Yang,Zining Zhong,Qian Cao,Jindong Li,Boyun Xu,Kaishen Yuan,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StorySpark，一个面向故事前提生成的模块化进化搜索框架。它将前提生成视为在可解释叙事模块（如背景、人物、事件、结局和转折）上的局部搜索过程，通过生成备选、上下文评估、反馈驱动的变异与重组、Pareto引导的选择以及前沿能力再分配，生成更高质量且更具原创性的故事前提，并能由此产出更优质的下游故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM故事生成研究多聚焦于后期规划、可控性、连贯性和散文扩展阶段，而前提层面的创意构思相对未被充分探索。故事前提是整个叙事的创意火花，但现有方法缺少对前提生成这一关键环节的系统化研究。

Method: StorySpark采用模块化进化搜索框架：将故事前提分解为背景、人物、事件、结局、转折等可解释模块；每个活跃模块被视为一个局部搜索空间，其条件由已构建的部分前提决定；对每个模块生成多个备选方案并在上下文中评估，通过反馈驱动的变异与重组进行精炼；利用Pareto引导的选择保留互补优势，并重新分配前沿容量以平衡分支覆盖与有前景的方向。

Result: 多视角自动评估和人类评估表明，StorySpark生成的前提优于竞争基线，尤其在原创性方面表现一致；当使用相同的故事写作者扩展前提时，StorySpark的前提能产生更高质量的后续故事，同时保持完整性、吸引力以及多样化的可用叙事方向。

Conclusion: StorySpark验证了通过模块化进化搜索进行前提级创意构思的有效性，为LLM故事生成中的早期创意阶段提供了一种系统化方法，填补了前提生成研究空白，并能正向传导至下游故事质量。

Abstract: A story premise is the creative spark from which a full narrative can grow. Yet LLM-based story generation has mostly emphasized later-stage planning, controllability, coherence, and prose expansion, while premise-level ideation remains comparatively underexplored. We introduce StorySpark, a module-wise evolutionary search framework for story premise generation. StorySpark operates over interpretable narrative modules such as background, persona, event, ending, and twist, treating each active module not as a static field to fill once, but as a local search space conditioned on the partial premise built so far. For each module, it generates alternatives, evaluates them in context, refines them through feedback-driven mutation and recombination, preserves complementary strengths with Pareto-guided selection, and reallocates frontier capacity to balance branch coverage with promising directions. Multi-view automatic and human evaluations show that StorySpark produces stronger final premises than competitive baselines, with especially consistent gains in originality; when expanded with the same story writer, its premises also lead to higher-quality downstream stories while maintaining completeness, fascination, and diverse usable narrative directions.

</details>


### [15] [From Refuse to Richness: Rubric Rewards for Long-Form Hallucination Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12337)
*Yudong Wang,Zhe Yang,Wenhan Ma,Rang Li,Qibin Yang,Weimin Xiong,Jiangshan Duo,Liang Zhao,Zhifang Sui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究长文本生成中的幻觉RL训练中"拒绝回答"与"内容丰富度"之间的权衡，提出使用关键点评分标准（key-point rubric）替代全局内容丰富度代理指标（如长度、数量等），并发现将接地（grounding）、rubric覆盖度和相关性进行软组合的奖励函数能取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惩罚无依据声明的奖励可以提高长文本生成的接地性，但也会导致模型倾向于少回答（拒绝式回答），造成"拒绝-丰富度"权衡问题。现有研究使用长度、声明数量、细节或成对相关性等全局丰富度代理指标，无法直接定义回答应覆盖的内容，因此需要更精确地表达覆盖度的方法。

Method: 用关键点评分标准（key-point rubric）表示每个问题，该标准明确指定有用回答应覆盖的必需信息和可选信息。Rubrics既用于评估又用作奖励信号。实验中比较了四种奖励方案：仅接地（grounding-only）、基于代理指标（proxy-based）、仅rubric（rubric-only）和组合（combined）奖励。

Result: 发现了稳定的权衡：严格的接地奖励改善支撑性但抑制覆盖度；无约束的rubric奖励改善覆盖但削弱接地。将接地、rubric覆盖度和相关性进行软组合的奖励在实验中取得了最佳平衡，改善了分布内支撑性，同时在分布外的checklist任务上比仅接地或仅rubric奖励具有更好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 关键点评分标准能够直接定义覆盖度，而软组合的奖励函数（接地+rubric覆盖度+相关性）能够有效平衡长文本生成中的接地性和内容丰富度，并且具有更好的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Rewards that penalize unsupported claims can improve grounding in long-form generation, but they can also teach models to answer less. We study this refusal-to-richness trade-off in long-form hallucination RL. Instead of using global richness proxies such as length, claim count, detail, or pairwise relevance, we represent each question with a key-point rubric that specifies the required and optional information a useful answer should cover. These rubrics define coverage directly and are used both for evaluation and as reward signals. Across grounding-only, proxy-based, rubric-only, and combined rewards, we find a stable trade-off: strict grounding rewards improve support but suppress coverage, while unconstrained rubric rewards improve coverage but weaken grounding. A soft combination of grounding, rubric coverage, and relevance gives the best balance in our experiments, improving in-distribution support while transferring better to out-of-distribution checklist tasks than either grounding-only or rubric-only rewards.

</details>


### [16] [SDAM: Structure-Difference-Aware Memory Evolution for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12338)
*Keyan Xu,Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SDAM，一种基于结构差异感知和模式锚定记忆演进的Text-to-SQL方法，通过三阶段机制提升复杂SQL生成质量，在BIRD-dev和Spider-test上分别取得2.0和0.4的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于记忆的agent系统在Text-to-SQL任务中虽然能提升复杂SQL生成能力，但存在三个关键缺陷：忽略历史经验、结构分析能力弱、语义理解浅层以及模式（schema）对齐不佳，导致生成SQL的准确性和鲁棒性不足。

Method: SDAM包含三个核心组件：1) 结构差异感知推理树（structure-difference aware reasoning tree），用于识别潜在错误；2) 矛盾感知反思（contradiction-aware reflection），用于提取深层语义规则；3) 模式锚定记忆演进机制（schema-grounded memory evolution），将记忆与数据库模式绑定以增强结构一致性。SDAM被集成到一个称为SDAM-SQL的完整Text-to-SQL框架中。

Result: 在BIRD-dev和Spider-test基准上，SDAM-SQL相比主流Text-to-SQL方法分别取得了2.0和0.4的准确率提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SDAM通过结构感知、矛盾反思和模式锚定记忆三种机制互补，有效解决了现有记忆系统在Text-to-SQL中的结构分析与模式对齐问题，显著提升了复杂SQL生成性能。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to convert natural language questions into executable SQL queries. While memory-based agent system improves complex SQL generation, existing memory design neglect historical experience and suffer from weak structure analysis, shallow semantic understanding, and poor schema alignment. To address these challenges, we propose SDAM. Specifically, SDAM identifies potential errors via a structure-difference aware reasoning tree, extracts deep semantic rules through contradiction-aware reflection, and enhances structural consistency using a schema-grounded memory evolution mechanism to bind memory with database schemas. We integrate SDAM into a Text-to-SQL framework named SDAM-SQL. Experiment shows that SDAM-SQL achieves 2.0 and 0.4 improvement on BIRD-dev and Spider-test compared with mainstream Text-to-SQL methods, showing the effectiveness of SDAM-SQL.

</details>


### [17] [Mimicry without understanding: the origins of decision bias in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12339)
*Eldad Yechiam,Adi Tarabeih*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）中社会、情感和认知偏见产生的两种机制：一是对基于人类行为的偏好进行错误模仿（即使行为与偏好逻辑无关），二是对明确存在偏见的人类行为进行模仿。通过四项经济偏见研究，发现ChatGPT-4o和Qwen在摄取非指示性人类行为报告时表现出社会证明偏见，且当损失厌恶被明确描述为偏误时，LLM也会表现出该偏见，科学报告中偏见的程度能预测LLM后续的偏见表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被发现容易受到多种社会、情感和认知偏见的影响，但以往研究主要聚焦于训练数据本身存在偏见的情况。作者希望探究即使人类偏好并不存在偏见或已被正确分类为偏见时，LLM偏见仍然产生的底层机制，从而深化对LLM偏见成因的理解。

Method: 通过四项以经济偏见为核心的行为实验，分别考察两种机制：(1) 基于人类行为的错误偏好模仿——测试LLM在面对逻辑上与偏好无关的行为报告时，是否仍会推断出人类偏好并产生社会证明偏见；(2) 明确偏见的模仿——测试当损失厌恶被明确描述为偏误时，LLM是否仍然表现出该偏见，并分析科学报告中偏见程度与LLM后续偏见表现之间的预测关系。

Result: ChatGPT-4o和Qwen在接收到明确非指示性的人类行为报告时，仍表现出社会证明偏见；LLM在损失厌恶被明确描述为偏见时也表现出该偏见，且科学报告中损失厌恶的严重程度能显著预测LLM自身后续的偏见表现。

Conclusion: 科学研究中关于偏见的报告可能成为自我实现的预言——至少在LLM的回应中如此。本研究超越了单纯列举LLM偏见的现象层面，揭示了偏见产生的底层组成过程，为理解和缓解LLM偏见提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Large Language models (LLMs) were found to be susceptible to a host of social, affective, and cognitive biases. We examined two mechanisms through which such biases can be generated even when human preferences (in the training data) are not biased or when they are correctly categorized as being biased. The first is faulty mimicry of preferences based on human behavior: this involves LLMs inferring human preferences even when behaviors are logically unrelated to preferences. The second is mimicry of explicitly biased human behaviors. In four studies focusing on economic biases, we find that ChatGPT-4o and Qwen exhibited social proof biases even when prompted with reports of human behaviors that were clearly non-indicative of individuals' actual preferences. LLMs also displayed loss aversion when it was explicitly described as a bias. Indeed, when prompted with detailed scientific reports, the extent of the bias (i.e., loss aversion) in the scientific report predicted LLMs' own subsequent bias. Scientific papers of biases can thus become self-fulfilling prophecies, at least when it comes to LLMs' responses. The current study goes beyond fleshing out LLM biases and sheds light on the underlying component processes.

</details>


### [18] [Class-Structure Preservation Beats Diversity: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Text Augmentation Methods for Imbalanced Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12340)
*Keito Inoshita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含11种数据增强方法的受控基准，在7个文本分类数据集上系统比较了基于LLM的增强与嵌入空间SMOTE式检索（EmbSMOTE）方法，发现所有LLM方法在统计上等价或劣于EmbSMOTE，且性能差距随类别不平衡程度增大而扩大，证明影响效果的关键变量是类条件结构保真度而非表面多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式数据增强在不平衡文本分类中受到广泛关注，但目前缺乏将LLM增强与强经典的嵌入空间SMOTE式检索（EmbSMOTE）进行系统对比的经验基准，无法回答LLM增强是否真正优于经典方法这一核心问题。

Method: 在7个公开文本分类数据集（类别数K=2-28，不平衡比1.1至500+）上构建受控基准，比较11种增强方法（覆盖经典扰动、嵌入空间检索和LLM生成），每个单元格使用5个随机种子，采用macro F1、Welch's t检验、5种分布度量进行评估，并基于Qwen3-8B进行LLM家族敏感性分析。

Result: 所有LLM增强方法在统计上等价或劣于EmbSMOTE，性能差距随不平衡程度增加而单调扩大，在GoEmotions-28上达到ΔF1_macro≈0.063。表面层面唯一性与下游性能相关性可忽略，而LLM特有伪影（文本延长和标签分布均匀化）与分类精度负相关。

Conclusion: 决定增强效果的关键因素是类条件结构保真度（增强样本对训练分布类条件几何的保持程度），而非表面多样性。建议将基于检索的过采样作为不平衡多类分类的默认方法，并应在LLM增强实际部署前设立更高的经验标准。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), generative data augmentation has attracted considerable attention for imbalanced text classification in natural language processing. However, no empirical benchmark to date has compared LLM-based augmentation against the embedding-space SMOTE-style retrieval (EmbSMOTE), a strong classical reference for imbalanced classification. In this study, a controlled benchmark of 11 augmentation methods, spanning classical perturbation, embedding-space retrieval, and LLM-based generation, is newly constructed on seven public text classification datasets covering class counts $K=2$-$28$ and imbalance ratios of 1.1 to over 500, evaluated with five random seeds per cell using macro F1, Welch's $t$-tests, five distributional metrics, and an LLM-family sensitivity analysis based on Qwen3-8B. The experimental results reveal that all LLM-based methods are statistically equivalent or inferior to EmbSMOTE, with the performance gap widening monotonically as imbalance increases and reaching $Δ\text{F1}_\text{macro}\!\approx\!0.063$ on GoEmotions-28. Furthermore, it is observed that surface-level uniqueness has negligible correlation with downstream performance, whereas LLM-specific artifacts, such as text elongation and label-distribution uniformization, are negatively associated with classification accuracy. Compared with six LLM-based and four classical augmentation baselines, these results demonstrate that the effective variable is not surface-level diversity but class-conditional structural fidelity, namely the degree to which augmented samples preserve the class-conditioned geometry of the training distribution. Accordingly, retrieval-based oversampling should be adopted as the default for imbalanced multi-class classification, and a higher empirical bar should be required before LLM-based augmentation is deployed in practice.

</details>


### [19] [The "Knowledge-Behavior Gap" in Cultural Taboo Safety of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12341)
*Ying He,Sihang Jiang,Xingzhou Chen,Zhouhong Gu,Yiwei Gu,Minggui He,Shimin Tao,Hongxia Ma,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CulShield是首个专门评估和改进大语言模型文化禁忌安全性的公开基准，覆盖77个国家和地区、超2020个禁忌，同时评估显式知识与隐式行为，揭示模型存在"知识-行为鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化基准主要评估文化知识或价值偏见，忽视了模型能否识别和尊重文化禁忌，尤其是当禁忌隐式嵌入看似无害的问题中时。此外，文化禁忌具有隐式和上下文依赖的特性，给可靠评估带来独特挑战。

Method: 构建覆盖77个国家和地区的102020+禁忌的基准CulShield，分别从显式知识和隐式行为两个维度评估模型的文化禁忌安全性，并通过改变语言上下文来测试模型表现的稳定性。

Result: 在GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.5-pro等先进LLM上的实验揭示出明显的"知识-行为鸿沟"：模型在交互中常常无法应用已知的禁忌知识。此外，语言上下文的变化会显著影响模型的文化禁忌安全性。

Conclusion: CulShield填补了文化禁忌安全评估的空白，证明LLM虽具备文化禁忌知识但在实际交互中应用不足，且对语言上下文敏感，为后续改进模型的文化安全性提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: Cultural taboo safety is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs), as culturally insensitive outputs may cause offense or even social harm. However, existing cultural benchmarks primarily assess cultural knowledge or values biases, while overlooking whether LLMs can recognize and respect cultural taboos, especially when taboos are implicitly hidden in seemingly harmless questions. Besides, cultural taboos are implicit, and context-dependent, thus poss unique challenges for reliable evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce \textbf{CulShield}, the first public benchmark dedicated to evaluating and improving the cultural taboo safety of LLMs. CulShield spans 77 countries and territories, and includes over 2,020 taboos. It evaluates models along both explicit knowledge and implicit behaviors. Experiments on several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-pro) reveal a clear ``knowledge-behavior gap'': models often fail to apply known taboos during interaction. We further show that variations in linguistic context can significantly affect LLMs' cultural taboo safety. Code and data is accessible here: https://github.com/hedyHe/CulShield.

</details>


### [20] [Are Large Language Models Reliable Reviewers? A Benchmark for Error Detection in Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12342)
*Ying He,Zhouhong Gu,Zhecheng Hu,Yubo Zhou,Hao Shen,Jiaqing Liang,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Yanghua Xiao,Zhixu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinED-Bench，这是首个用于金融错误检测的公开基准测试，涵盖三个认知复杂度级别、九个真实金融场景及900多份2025年报告。实验表明当前LLMs在该任务上仍表现不佳，尤其在高复杂度案例中，但监督微调可显著提升较弱LLMs的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在股票价格预测、金融分析等多个金融任务上表现出色，但其识别金融文档错误的能力尚未被探索。金融文档的准确性对经济分析、监管合规和企业决策至关重要，因此有必要系统评估LLMs在这一关键任务上的表现。

Method: 构建FinED-Bench基准测试：覆盖三个认知复杂度级别和九个真实世界金融场景，包含900多份2025年报告（对现有LLMs不可见），详细描述基准构建流程，并评估多个先进LLMs（如GPT-4o、Qwen3-14B），同时进行监督微调实验以检验其效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前LLMs在金融错误检测任务上仍面临困难，尤其在高复杂度案例中表现不佳；监督微调可以显著提升较弱LLMs在该任务上的性能。

Conclusion: FinED-Bench填补了金融错误检测领域基准测试的空白，揭示了当前LLMs在此任务上的局限性，并证明监督微调是提升模型性能的有效路径。该基准可为未来金融文档验证和审计相关研究提供重要评估框架。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy of financial documents is critical for economic analysis, regulatory compliance, and corporate decision-making. Several studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) perform well in many financial tasks, such as stock price movements and financial analytics. However, a critical task remains unexplored: the ability of LLMs to identify errors in financial documents. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{FinED-Bench}, the first publicly \textbf{Bench}mark for \textbf{Fin}ancial \textbf{E}rror \textbf{D}etection across three levels of cognitive complexity. FinED-Bench covers nine real-world financial scenarios, and includes over 900 documents reported in 2025 that are unseen by existing language models. We detail the benchmark construction process and evaluate several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Qwen3-14B) on this tasks, which requires both financial domain knowledge and reasoning capabilities. Experimental results show that current LLMs still struggle with this task, especially in high-complexity cases. Besides, supervised fine-tuning can significantly improve the performance of weaker LLMs on this task. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/hedyHe/FinED-Bench.

</details>


### [21] [Large Language Models Pass the History Exam But Miss the <<History>>: A Polish High School Exit Exam Matura Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12343)
*Adrian Trzoss,Kacper Dudzic,Wiktor Werner,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次构建基于波兰国家课程体系的历史学科LLM基准测试，评估八种主流大语言模型在波兰高中毕业会考（Matura）历史科目中的表现，发现所有模型均大幅超越人类考生，但存在任务类型、来源模态和地理范围上的排名不稳定性，且模型对波兰历史内容存在一致性的表现偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI聊天机器人被学生广泛用作知识来源，但现有的LLM基准测试很少评估解释性历史推理能力。历史学科要求的是对史料的批判性分析而非简单的事实检索，需要专门的基准来系统评估模型在此类学术任务中的表现。

Method: 选取2023-2025年三套波兰高中历史会考官方试卷（含简答题和论述题），对八种主流LLM进行测试，将模型表现与人类考生群体进行对比，同时从任务类型、来源模态、地理范围三个维度分析模型表现的差异，并通过定性错误分析揭示常见的失败模式。

Result: 所有模型在总分上均大幅超过人类考生，但汇总分数掩盖了模型能力的显著差异：模型在不同任务类型、来源模态和地理范围间的排名不稳定，且整体上对波兰历史内容的得分系统性低于全球历史内容。定性错误分析发现两种反复出现的失败模式：来源混淆（模型从史料内容本身进行推理而非将史料作为分析对象）和时间迷失（回答在历史时间定位上出现错位）。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立了基于波兰国家课程的历史学科LLM基准，揭示了模型在总分优势掩盖下的能力结构性缺陷，表明LLM在解释性历史推理任务中仍存在关键的认知短板，这些发现对评估模型在人文教育场景中的适用性具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: AI chatbots are widely used by students as knowledge sources, yet LLM benchmarks rarely assess interpretative historical reasoning. We evaluate eight leading LLMs on the Polish high school exit exams (Matura) in history - three official papers from 2023-2025, comprising short-answer questions and extended essays - comparing model performance against the human examinee population. Every model dramatically outperforms human examinees, yet aggregate scores mask distinct competency profiles: rankings are unstable across task type, source modality, and geographical scope, with a consistent penalty on Polish versus Global history content. Qualitative error analysis reveals two recurring failure modes - source conflation, in which models reason from source content rather than treating it as an object of analysis, and temporal disorientation, in which responses are historically misplaced. This study introduces the first LLM history benchmark grounded in Polish national curriculum.

</details>


### [22] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGSieve是一个无需可信参考语料或攻击标记的自参考检索增强生成（RAG）投毒检测框架，通过查询局部对比（RSQ）和语料局部对比（RSG）两种机制，在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造下分别达到95.2%和93.3%的AUROC，联合部署可将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统将外部语料作为推理证据，攻击者可通过注入文档推广其选择的观点。现有检测方法依赖可信参考、特定攻击痕迹或对语料拓扑敏感的全局阈值，实用性受限。

Method: 提出自参考检测框架RAGSieve，构造参考来自被检系统本身。包含两个组件：RSQ在查询时对前5个候选与同次检索排名6-20的结果进行对比评分，检测答案锚点集中度和载体转移；RSG在语料摄入时对每个文档的语义相似但词面不同的邻居与其局部基线进行对比，检测投毒文档的协同密度。

Result: 在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造下，RSQ达到95.2% AUROC，在5%清洁文档去除下检出82.2%投毒（对比GMTP的81.1%/52.5%）；RSG达到93.3%/79.8%（对比CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%）。联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%，同时在未投毒检索上保留41.3%的F1分数。

Conclusion: RAGSieve在语料摄入和查询时间两个阶段提供实际保护，无需投毒标签或可信语料，展示了自参考检测框架在RAG安全领域的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [23] [Predicting consumer-technology ownership without a diffusion history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12344)
*Irina Vartanova,Niels Selling,Jennifer Viberg Johansson,Pontus Strimling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究测试消费者技术感知属性是否能预测其普及率。通过2022年Prolific调查（n=678）让人类对65种消费技术按六个属性评分，并让两个前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5）给出相同评分。基于UTAUT2四个接受属性加对数年龄协变量进行符号约束惩罚回归，逐一留出技术进行评估。属性模型在人类评分下比"上市年限"基线降低17%的平均绝对误差，大语言模型评分改进更大（Opus 4.7最优）。但在2022-2025年短期窗口内，属性无法优于无变化基线。论文讨论了局限性（如语言模型评分可能反映先验知识而非独立属性推理），并展示了2025-2026年上市的11个产品的2027年普及率预测示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究消费者技术感知属性（如UTAUT2接受度属性）能否有效预测技术普及率，并验证大语言模型能否替代人工评分提供等值甚至更好的属性估计，从而为技术采用预测提供低成本的替代数据源。

Method: 使用2022年Prolific线上调查（678名美国成人）对65种消费技术在六个属性维度上评分，同时获取两个大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5）的对应评分。以UTAUT2的四个接受属性加对数年龄为预测变量，采用符号约束惩罚回归建模普及率，通过每次留出一个技术的方式评估模型性能，并以"上市年限"基线和不变化基线作为对照。

Result: 属性模型在人类评分下较"上市年限"基线的平均绝对误差降低17%；大语言模型评分的改进更大，其中Opus 4.7表现最佳。在2022-2025年普及率变动很小的短期窗口内，属性无法优于无变化基线。论文同时给出了11个新产品的2027年普及率预测示例。

Conclusion: 感知属性对跨技术的普及率横截面差异具有预测能力，且大语言模型评分可有效替代人类评分，但该方法的预测优势主要体现在较长的时间跨度上，对短期波动预测能力有限。研究同时指出关键局限：语言模型评分可能只是编码了技术已有的先验知识，而非真正独立地进行属性推理。

Abstract: We test whether the perceived attributes of a consumer technology predict how widely it is owned. In a 2022 Prolific survey of US adults (n = 678), respondents rated 65 consumer technologies on six attributes. We then elicited the same ratings from two frontier language models, Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7 and OpenAI GPT-5.5. We regress ownership prevalence on four UTAUT2 acceptance attributes plus a log-age covariate with a sign-constrained penalized regression and evaluate it by holding out one technology at a time. The attribute model improves on a baseline of years-since-launch: mean absolute error falls by 17% with the human ratings, and by more with either model, most with Opus 4.7. Over the short 2022-to-2025 window, where ownership moved little, the same attributes do not improve on a no-change baseline. We set out the limitations of the approach, including the possibility that language-model ratings reflect prior knowledge of these technologies rather than independent attribute reasoning. We include a deployment illustration: 2027 ownership predictions for eleven products launched in 2025 and 2026.

</details>


### [24] [New Terms, New Toxicity: Consensus-based Chinese Neologism Toxicity Detection via Search-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12361)
*Shiyao Cui,QingLin Zhang,Di Wang,Yida Lu,Zhexin Zhang,Jinhua Gao,Jinglin Yang,Min He,Han Qiu,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何检测通过新词（neologisms）表达的隐性毒性内容，提出一个覆盖新词来源与共识验证标准的分类体系，构建相关词库，并引入SeTox框架——一种搜索增强方法，使静态大语言模型能够整合实时网络上下文来检测新词毒性。实验表明，即使使用3B规模的模型，SeTox也优于近期的大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新词（如"country girl"被用来污名化女权主义）看起来无害但已演变为公共共识中的毒性用法，对现有内容审核系统构成挑战，但该问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1) 提出一个分类体系（taxonomy），捕捉有毒新词的起源及共识验证标准；2) 构建覆盖广泛风险类别的新词词库；3) 提出SeTox（Search-augmented Toxicity detection），一种搜索增强框架，使静态大语言模型能够整合实时网络上下文用于新词毒性检测。

Result: SeTox即使使用3B规模的小模型，也优于近期的大规模模型，展示了其可扩展性，能够整合现实世界知识进行有毒新词检测。

Conclusion: SeTox通过引入实时网络搜索上下文，有效解决了静态LLM无法捕捉新词毒性语义漂移的问题，为隐性毒性内容检测提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Neologisms, emerging terms in meaning or form, can serve as new vehicles for toxic expression, like "country girl" as a stigmatizing label targeting feminism. Such toxic neologisms appear benign but have evolved into toxic usage in public consensus, posing challenges to moderation systems and remaining underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how to detect implicit toxicity expressed via neologisms. We first propose a taxonomy that captures the origins and consensus-verification criteria of toxic neologisms, followed by the construction of a lexicon spanning widely observed risk categories. To capture toxicity grounded in public consensus, we introduce SeTox, a search-augmented framework that enables static large language models (LLMs) to incorporate real-time web context for neologism toxicity detection. Experiments show that SeTox, even with 3B-scale models, outperforms recent large-scale models, demonstrating its scalability to incorporate real-world knowledge for toxic neologism detection. Disclaimer: this paper has offensive contents that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [25] [Are you Talking Logic to Me? Assessing Language Models Syllogistic Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12374)
*Hanna Abi Akl,Fabien Gandon,Catherine Faron,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同知识表示（KR）形式化符号对语言模型三段论推理能力的影响，通过扩展FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，在小语言模型上对比了SFT和零样本设置下的表现，并提出了一种三段论分类方法（SEF）来增强推理，同时开源了首个自动生成KR符号三段论的Python库CLGC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在三段论等逻辑推理任务上表现不佳，而知识表示（KR）在表达输入信息方面对模型求解任务具有关键作用。作者因此希望系统研究不同形式化KR符号对三段论推理性能的影响，以探索提升小模型逻辑推理能力的途径。

Method: 作者通过扩展FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，引入了多种形式化KR符号表示；在小语言模型上进行了有监督微调（SFT）和零样本（ZS）两种设置下的实验；提出了一种三段论分类方法（SEF），用于为零样本提示补充逻辑定义以增强推理；并开发了Python库CLGC，用于自动生成KR符号形式的三段论并定义其SEF类别。

Result: 实验结果表明，输入符号的选择可以在小语言模型上达到与自然语言相当的性能，同时实现更快的推理速度；SEF分类方法增强的零样本提示能有效提升小模型的推理能力。

Conclusion: 形式化KR符号可以作为自然语言的替代输入在小模型中实现有竞争力的推理性能且推理速度更快；SEF分类增强可提升零样本推理效果；CLGC库为后续研究提供了开源工具支持。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) struggle with logical tasks like reasoning on syllogisms. It has been shown that Knowledge Representation (KR) plays a crucial role in expressing input information to help models solve tasks. This observation motivates our study of the impact of different formal KR notations on syllogistic reasoning by extending the FOLIO and P-FOLIO datasets. Our experiments on Small Language Models (SLMs) in Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Zero-Shot (ZS) settings show that the choice of input notation can yield performances competitive with natural language while enabling faster inference. We also propose a syllogistic categorization method (SEF) and use it to enrich ZS prompts with logical definitions, which boost reasoning in small models. We open-source our framework, Common Logic Grammar Construction (CLGC), as the first Python library for automatically generating syllogisms in KR notations and defining their SEF categories.

</details>


### [26] [Query Timing Produces Opposite Positional Biases Between LLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12387)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在评估过程中表现出位置性偏差（如首因和近因效应），其产生机制与人类不同，且新模型中这些偏差更加严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLMs存在位置性偏差，但其背后的机制尚不清楚；同时，人类的首因/近因偏差与信息更新时机有关，作者希望探究LLM是否遵循类似规律。

Method: 研究LLM在接收证据的过程中是逐步更新信念还是仅在最后更新，并将其与人类行为进行对比分析。

Result: 发现LLM的信念更新机制与人类存在分歧，且较新的模型比前代模型表现出更明显的位置性偏差。

Conclusion: LLM的位置性偏差并非简单复刻人类机制，其偏差在新模型中反而加剧，暗示这一现象值得进一步深入研究和理解。

Abstract: Positional biases such as recency and primacy effects have been documented in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying mechanism by which these models make their evaluations remains poorly understood. Both primacy and recency biases have been observed in human judgments in response to evidence, but recent work suggest that \emph{when} the listener updates their beliefs -- during the presentation of evidence or only at the end -- influences the presence of such effects. We investigate whether a similar phenomenon holds for LLMs, finding divergence from human behavior. These biases are more exacerbated in newer models compared to their predecessors.

</details>


### [27] [Unified Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Complex Graph Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12391)
*Fali Wang,Ali Al-Lawati,Iliyas Bektas,Jinxuan Fang,Alek Melenski,Tianxiang Zhao,Yao Ma,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphGym，一个五阶段半自动框架用于构建复杂图推理基准测试，覆盖202个任务，在文本、代码和增强推理三种模式下评估LLM的图推理能力，揭示现有模型在多维度复杂性下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图推理基准存在三个主要局限：数据复杂性覆盖有限、过度依赖人工构建、缺乏统一的文本与代码推理模式评估。图推理作为评估LLM推理能力的理想测试平台，需要一个更全面、可扩展且自动化的基准来揭示模型真正的推理能力边界。

Method: 提出GraphGym框架，采用五阶段半自动构建流程：利用LLM作为数据生成器自动产生任务描述、图数据、参考解、图加载脚本、问题形式和评估脚本，同时保留关键质量控制环节的人工验证。基准沿五个维度扩展覆盖面：图规模、任务复杂性、任务描述、图加载方式和任务来源。基于此构建202个任务的基准，并在文本推理、代码推理和增强推理三种设置下评估LLM。

Result: 实验表明复杂性维度能够揭示现有基准中不可见的模型局限性；现有微调模型难以泛化到GraphGym；检索增强方法具有场景依赖的适应性——能改进文本推理但未一致提升代码推理性能。

Conclusion: GraphGym作为兼具挑战性和诊断性的图推理基准，为未来图推理增强方法提供了实证指导，代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Graph reasoning provides a promising testbed for evaluating the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs), as graph instances can be programmatically generated, structurally controlled, and naturally scaled to long-input settings. However, existing graph reasoning benchmarks have limited coverage of data complexity, rely heavily on manual construction, and lack unified evaluation across text-based and code-based reasoning modes. To address these limitations, we propose {\dataset}, a five-stage \textit{semi-automatic} framework for constructing complex graph reasoning benchmarks. It expands benchmark coverage along five dimensions: \textit{Graph Size}, \textit{Task Complexity}, \textit{Task Description}, \textit{Graph Loading}, and \textit{Task Source}. The framework uses an LLM-based data generator to automatically produce task descriptions, graph data, reference solutions, graph-loading scripts, question forms, and evaluation scripts, while retaining human validation at key quality-control stages. Based on it, we construct a benchmark with $202$ tasks and evaluate LLMs under text-based, code-based, and augmented reasoning settings. Experiments show that the complexity dimensions reveal model limitations that are less visible in existing benchmarks; existing fine-tuned models struggle to generalize to GraphGym, whereas retrieval-augmented methods show scenario-dependent adaptability, improving textual reasoning but not consistently improving coding reasoning. These findings suggest that ours serves as a challenging and diagnostic benchmark for graph reasoning and provides empirical guidance for future enhancement methods. Code and dataset will be published soon.

</details>


### [28] [DIVE: Unlocking Self-Improvement in Frozen Language Models Through Diversity-Driven Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12486)
*Siheng Xiong,Ali Payani,Oguzhan Gungordu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DIVE框架，通过多样性驱动的自然语言技能演化，使冻结的大语言模型无需参数更新即可从任务经验中自我改进。DIVE并行演化多个技能种群，通过多样化变换和互补选择来缓解优化方差，在数学和逻辑推理任务中显著优于现有多类基线方法，且技能可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型无法在不进行参数更新的情况下保留部署后获得的经验。虽然可以通过提示工程或训练来提升模型能力，但冻结模型缺少一种可扩展的机制来从任务经验中持续自我改进。此外，自然语言技能演化是一个随机、非凸的搜索过程，单条技能轨迹容易过拟合或收敛到次优解，需要一种能有效缓解优化方差的方法。

Method: DIVE框架的核心在于：1）从引导经验中独立演化多个技能种群；2）通过多样化的变换（如推导可重用推理过程、验证策略、常见失败模式、输出约束）自适应细化技能；3）联合选择互补的技能集合。整个过程由同一底层冻结模型执行和修订，无需教师模型，通过演化多个并行的技能轨迹来降低随机搜索的方差。

Result: 在六个数学和逻辑推理任务及多个模型家族上，DIVE始终优于现有推理方法、提示优化方法、技能开发框架和基于记忆的基线。相比基于参数的方法（如SFT、GRPO）和提示优化（GEPA），DIVE以更少的采样获得更大的性能提升。此外，学到的技能可跨模型尺度和家族迁移，使较小的模型（如GPT-5-nano）在常规提示下达到或超过较大模型（如GPT-5）的性能。

Conclusion: 多样性驱动的技能演化被证明是一种有效、可解释且无需参数的LLM自我改进方法。它通过并行演化多个技能种群并选择互补技能集合，既缓解了优化方差问题，又实现了技能的跨模型可迁移性，为冻结大模型的持续自我进化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) cannot retain post-deployment experience without parameter updates. We introduce DIVE, a diversity-driven framework that enables frozen LLMs to improve by evolving persistent natural-language skills from task experience and verifier feedback. These skills encode reusable reasoning procedures, verification strategies, common failure modes, and output constraints and are both executed and revised by the same underlying model without access to a teacher model. Since natural-language skill evolution is a stochastic, non-convex search process, optimizing a single skill trajectory can overfit to sampled experience or converge to a suboptimal solution. DIVE mitigates this optimization variance by independently evolving multiple skill populations from bootstrapped experience, adaptively refining them through diverse transformations, and jointly selecting a complementary set of skills. Across six mathematical and logical reasoning tasks and multiple model families, DIVE consistently outperforms existing reasoning methods, prompt-optimization approaches, skill-development frameworks, and memory-based baselines. It achieves rapid self-improvement from accumulated experience, obtaining substantially larger performance gains with fewer rollouts than parameter-based methods such as SFT and GRPO, and prompt optimization with GEPA. Further, the resulting skills transfer across model scales and families, enabling smaller models such as GPT-5-nano to match or outperform larger counterparts, i.e., GPT-5, under conventional prompting. These results establish diversity-driven skill evolution as an effective, interpretable, and parameter-free approach to LLM self-improvement.

</details>


### [29] [Intensional Anaphora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12598)
*Ezra Keshet,Steven Abney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文讨论内涵语境下的照应（anaphora）问题。传统观点将内涵算子视为对可能世界的量词，但内涵语境中引入的个体难以作为后续代词的先行词（如上例"Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger"中的cheeseburger无法被后续代词指称）。Stone (1999)等人提出代词值（referents）须在世界中实际存在的约束来排除此类照应。本文通过反例表明该约束既非充分也非必要，提出基于描述（description-based）而非基于值（value-based）的照应理论，并构建了新逻辑Plural Intensional Presuppositional predicate calculus（PIP）加以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论（Stone 1999; Stone & Hardt 1999; Brasoveanu 2010）通过要求代词的指称物必须在评价世界中存在来排除非真实内涵语境中的照应，但作者发现该约束与事实不符：存在指称物确实存在却仍不允许照应的情况，也存在指称物可能不存却允许照应的情况。因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 提出基于描述而非基于值的照应理论：代词预设其对应的先行词描述在上下文集（context set）的每个可能世界中都被实例化。例如"Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger"之后说"It is large"要成立，必须Andrea在每个候选世界中都在吃一个芝士汉堡。该理论通过新逻辑PIP（Plural Intensional Presuppositional predicate calculus）实现，构建于Keshet (2018)与Abney & Keshet (2022)之上。每个PIP公式可直接翻译为带集合抽象的标准一阶谓词演算，提供经典逻辑基础。

Result: 作者展示了基于值存在的约束既不能排除所有不允许的照应（over-generates），也不能允许所有可接受的照应（under-generates）。基于描述的方案能够正确预测上述两种情况，并通过PIP逻辑为描述性照应提供形式化的经典逻辑实现。

Conclusion: 内涵照应的合适刻画应采用描述性（description-based）而非值性（value-based）方案：代词预设先行词描述在每个候选世界中都被实例化。所提出的PIP逻辑为这一理论提供了可直接翻译为标准一阶逻辑的经典基础，是未来深入研究内涵照应的起点。

Abstract: Intensional operators are often treated as quantifiers over possible worlds, parallel to the treatment of determiners as quantifiers over individuals. Yet individuals introduced in intensional contexts cannot serve as antecedents to later pronouns as easily as those introduced in merely quantificational contexts. For instance, "Everyone is eating a cheeseburger" may be followed by "They are large", where "they" refers to the cheeseburgers being eaten. However, as Stone (1999) points out, the similar "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger" does not support later anaphoric references such as "It is large" or "They are large". Stone (1999), Stone and Hardt (1999), and Brasoveanu (2010) address this by requiring a pronoun's value (its referents) to exist in the world of evaluation, ruling out anaphora from non-veridical intensional contexts. We show, however, both cases where such anaphora is disallowed even when the pronoun's referents clearly exist and cases where it is allowed even though they might not exist. We argue that intensional anaphora is best captured using a description-based rather than value-based account. A pronoun presupposes that its corresponding antecedent description is instantiated in each world of the context set. Thus, there must be a cheeseburger being eaten by Andrea in every candidate world for "It is large" to be felicitous after "Andrea might be eating a cheeseburger". We implement our proposal via a new logic, building on Keshet (2018) and Abney and Keshet (2022), called Plural Intensional Presuppositional predicate calculus (PIP). Each PIP formula translates directly into standard first-order predicate calculus with set abstraction, providing a classical foundation for this work.

</details>


### [30] [When Explanations Betray Backdoors: Black-Box Auditing for Language Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12623)
*Yang Liu,Ran Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解释（rationale/evidence）的LLM分类器后门审计方法。在只有干净校准数据、无触发器信息但可获取标签加简短理由的条件下，提出了Groundness Drift分数——衡量答案摘要是否仍然基于输入。在两个7B骨干模型、五个数据集和四种常见非自适应OpenBackdoor风格攻击下，Groundness Drift在所有情况下均优于比较的检测器（更高AUROC、更低残余目标ASR，在5%干净FPR预算下）。此外提出了Unsupported Groundness多探针升级方案应对解释伪装压力场景，但该方案虽改善信号却未能完全弥合自适应攻击差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分类器常配备解释输出用于审核、路由、主题分诊和低资源标注等场景，但这些模型容易受到后门攻击。现有的黑盒审计方法往往需要触发器信息或大量标注数据，而在现实场景中防御者仅有干净的校准数据且无法获取触发器信息。本文旨在探索在更困难但更实际的设置下——仅能查询分类器获得标签和简短理由/引文证据——如何有效检测后门注入。

Method: 本文提出两个核心方法：(1) Groundedness Drift，一个轻量级分数，通过衡量答案摘要是否仍然基于输入（即groundedness是否发生漂移）来检测后门行为；(2) Unsupported Groundedness，一种多探针升级策略，针对解释伪装（explanation-camouflage）的压力场景，通过多个探测输入来强化检测信号。实验在2个7B骨干（backbone）、5个数据集上进行，覆盖4种常见的非自适应OpenBackdoor风格攻击家族。

Result: 在5%干净FPR预算下，Groundedness Drift在所有设置中均比所有对比检测器取得更高的AUROC和更低的残余目标ASR。Unsupported Groundedness进一步提升检测信号，但无法完全弥合与自适应攻击之间的差距（adaptive gap仍然存在）。

Conclusion: Groundedness Drift是一种有效且轻量的黑盒后门检测方法，适用于仅有干净校准数据和安全解释输出的场景。尽管多探针升级（Unsupported Groundedness）能改善对抗解释伪装的检测能力，但自适应攻击下的残余风险仍然存在，表明该方向仍需进一步研究以关闭自适应差距。

Abstract: Language model classifiers with explanations are used for moderation, routing, topic triage, and low-resource annotation. We study black-box auditing when the defender has only clean calibration data without trigger information but can ask the classifier for a label plus a short rationale or quoted evidence. We introduce Groundedness Drift, a lightweight score measuring whether the answer summary remains grounded in the input. Across two 7B backbones, five datasets, and four common non-adaptive OpenBackdoor-style attack families, Groundedness Drift achieves higher AUROC and lower residual target ASR than every compared detector in all cases at a nominal 5\% clean-FPR budget. We then evaluate Unsupported Groundedness, a multi-probe escalation for explanation-camouflage stress cases. Unsupported Groundedness improves signals but does not close the adaptive gap.

</details>


### [31] [LLMs Are Not Good Strategists, Yet Memory-Enhanced Agency Boosts Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12626)
*Yi Wu,Zhimin Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpicStar框架，通过将记忆作为策略来学习，使LLM智能体在长时间跨度环境中保持战略推理的一致性，显著超越基线方法并大幅降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时域环境中的战略推理因有限的注意力资源而难以维持连贯的子目标，导致"战略漂移"（strategic drift）问题——局部决策无法形成连贯的推理轨迹。现有方法缺乏有效的跨回合记忆机制来维持战略连贯性。

Method: EpicStar框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 成功历史回合记忆库作为启发式策略；(2) 工作记忆用于跟踪短期环境变化；(3) 动态门控机制，在推理时决定直接执行检索到的动作还是结合检索回合与当前工作记忆进行上下文融合推理。在StarCraft II环境中针对多样化对手风格进行评估。

Result: EpicStar在StarCraft II中显著优于基线方法，取得更高胜率的同时消耗约一个数量级更少的token，并且在不同难度和对手策略下均保持一致的优势。

Conclusion: 结构化跨回合记忆对于LLM智能体在动态自主环境中执行稳健的长期战略推理至关重要，记忆作为策略的学习范式为长时域LLM智能体提供了新的有效路径。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) within long-horizon environments is often limited by inconsistent subgoals. In these settings, finite attention resources prevent the model from maintaining strategic coherence over thousands of steps. This limitation leads to strategic drift, where localized decisions fail to sustain a coherent trajectory across reasoning. To address this, we introduce EpicStar, a framework that enables agents to learn memory as policy to tackle long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, the agent maintains a bank of successful past episodes as a heuristic alongside a working memory to track short-term environmental changes. During inference, a dynamic gating mechanism determines whether to execute a retrieved action directly or to perform new reasoning through a contextual fusion of the retrieved episodes and current working memory. Utilizing StarCraft II as the testbed, we evaluated EpicStar against diverse opponent styles. It significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving higher win rates while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and it maintains this advantage consistently across difficulty levels and opponent strategies. Our findings provide compelling evidence that structured cross-episode memory is essential for enabling LLM agents to perform robust, long-term strategic execution in dynamic, autonomous settings.

</details>


### [32] [Novels generated by language models show compressed formal variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12630)
*Mehdy Sedaghat Payam,Justin Quinn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了AI生成小说与人类小说在文体多样性上的差异，发现AI反复生成的小说在句子结构、可读性、标点使用等方面表现出明显的"方差闭合"（variance overclosure），即AI生 成的小说集合之间的文体差异远小于人类小说集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型可以生成整部小说，但关于其多次生成输出在正式变量上的变化程度知之甚少。现有研究多关注单篇文本是否可被识别为AI生成，而本研究关注的是：反复AI生成能否产生与人类语料库相当的多样性范围。

Method: 构建六个语料库进行对比：使用GPT-5.5 Thinking和Qwen3-14B分别生成二十部十九世纪英国现实主义风格小说及二十部当代零风格小说，并与205部十九世纪人类小说和65部当代人类零风格小说对比。在文档层面测量MATTR-500、Shannon熵、平均句长、可读性和标点率等指标。

Result: 最稳健的发现是句子结构的压缩：AI反复生成的小说在句法结构上的变异远小于人类小说。压缩现象也出现在可读性、标点和句长变异性等指标中。词法指标大多也被压缩，唯有Qwen零风格MATTR例外。GPT和Qwen虽然具有不同的平均风格特征，但缺乏稳定的跨指标相关性模式。

Conclusion: 该研究区分了"方差闭合"（小说间有限的形式范围）和"相关性闭合"（correlational overclosure）两种现象。个体AI生成的小说可能在风格上接近人类小说，但AI生成的小说集合整体占据的正式范围要窄得多。

Abstract: While large language models can generate entire novels, there is little information about the level of formal variation in their output over many generations. Rather than asking whether individual passages can be identified as AI-generated, this study asks whether repeated AI generation can produce the same range of diversity which is found across human corpora. This paper contrasts six corpora based on generation source and target style: twenty novels generated using GPT-5.5 Thinking in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using Qwen3-14B in a nineteenth-century British realist style, twenty novels generated using each of these models in a contemporary zero style, 205 nineteenth-century human-written British novels, and sixty-five contemporary human-written Zero-Style novels. At the document level, the research includes MATTR-500, Shannon entropy, average sentence length, readability, and punctuation rate measurements. The most robust and reliable result is compression of sentence structure. Repeated generations produce novels that vary far less from one another in sentence structure than human novels do. Compression is also present in the measures of readability, punctuation, and sentence length variability within novels. Lexical measures tend to be similarly compressed, with the exception of Qwen Zero-Style MATTR. Despite having distinct mean stylistic profiles, GPT and Qwen lack a stable pattern of cross-measure correlation. This article therefore distinguishes between variance overclosure, which represents a limited formal range between novels, and a more specific phenomenon of correlational overclosure. This means that an individual AI-generated novel may resemble human fiction stylistically, while a collection of AI-generated novels occupies a much narrower formal range.

</details>


### [33] [Excess Separability: Nuisance-Controlled Residual-Stream Probing for Benchmark Contamination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12652)
*Florian Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套稳健的激活探针法来检测基准测试数据污染（benchmark contamination），通过零和对比的深度探测曲线、水平匹配的安慰剂基线和标签置换零假设，有效控制了假阳性率，并在真实Transformer模型上验证了基线深度轮廓的非平坦性及其与表面差异的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准污染检测方法（n-gram重叠、基于似然的成员推断、金丝雀字符串）各有局限：需要训练语料库、需要精心选择的检验统计量、或需要在数据集发布时预先规划。近期提出的基于内部激活线性探针的方法虽有望解决这些问题，但作者发现其"自然实现方式"并不有效，因此需要设计一套能在测量噪声中存活的稳健协议。

Method: 提出一套系统化的探测协议：(1) 使用零和对比的深度轮廓（以水平匹配的安慰剂基线居中）；(2) 针对标签置换的零假设进行检验；(3) 参考集大小为可疑集的两倍。作者逐一测量并拒绝了多个更简单的替代方案，包括对比平坦深度轮廓、保持探针固定的item bootstrap、以及缩小一半的基线，证明每一个都会导致不同程度的假阳性或功效损失。

Result: 在真实Transformer上，基线深度轮廓显著非平坦，时间切分上跨度达29.1个准确率点，且非平坦程度与被测项集之间的表面差异相关（6次审计中相关系数0.87）。所有4个匹配良好的Pile数据组均返回零结果，协议对时间切分拒绝给出判定。假阳性率从0.03到0.99随分析者的对照组规模变化，平坦轮廓对比方法双向失效（真实零假设下72%的假阳性率或完全丧失功效）。

Conclusion: 该协议为激活探针法检测基准污染提供了可复现、统计上稳健的实现框架，但作者明确承认其局限：唯一的阳性结果出现在可交换性失效的切分上，因此本研究并不能证明Transformer是否真正携带"熟悉度方向"（familiarity direction）。代码、测试和审计均已发布。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination is diagnosed today with n-gram overlap, with likelihood-based membership inference, or with canary strings, and each needs something usually unavailable: the training corpus, a well-chosen test statistic, or foresight at dataset release. A recent alternative reads contamination off a linear probe on internal activations. We show that the natural way to do this does not work, and specify one that survives measurement.
  The protocol reports a zero-sum contrast on the depth profile of probe accuracy, recentred on a level-matched placebo baseline, tested against a label-permutation null, with the reference set twice the size of the suspect set. Each choice replaces a simpler alternative we measured and rejected. Reporting the level of excess separability rather than its shape makes the false positive rate track the size of the analyst's own control set, from 0.03 to 0.99 under a true null. Contrasting against a flat depth profile fails in both directions, rejecting a true null 0.72 of the time when surface decodability rises with depth and losing all power when it falls. An item bootstrap holds the fitted probe fixed and rejects up to 0.09 of the time where a permutation null that refits it holds 0.02. A half-size baseline triples the error rate.
  On real transformers, baseline depth profiles are measurably not flat, spanning up to 29.1 accuracy points on a temporal split, and their non-flatness tracks the surface difference between the item sets (correlation 0.87 over 6 audits), so the correction is largest exactly where it is needed. All 4 well-matched Pile arms return null, and the protocol refuses a verdict on the temporal split rather than reporting one. What this does not establish is whether transformers carry a familiarity direction at all: the only positive sits on the split where exchangeability fails. Implementation, tests and audits are released.

</details>


### [34] [ERSkill: Evolving for Skill-Guided Adaptive Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12720)
*Haolong Chen,Liang Zhang,Zhuo Li,Lei Xue,Guanrxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ERSkill框架，将大语言模型代理中的长期记忆检索视为可进化组件，通过可执行技能和动态路由实现自进化的技能引导记忆访问，在多个代理记忆基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理日益依赖长期记忆进行持久交互，但控制记忆的检索机制很少被视为可进化的组件。这种静态方法在处理异构记忆查询时表现受限，因为不同查询往往需要多样化的证据构建策略。

Method: ERSkill是一个以检索为中心的框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 将交互历史编译为结构化记忆存储，并将检索行为表示为由基本原语组成的可执行技能；(2) 在推理时，通过训练好的路由器动态将每个查询匹配到最优技能以构建证据；(3) 在训练过程中协同进化技能集和路由器，利用经验字典树高效记录已探索的检索路径，并采用双前沿机制安全地将新技能能力的扩展与面向路由器的稳定部署解耦。

Result: 在多个代理记忆基准上的实验表明，ERSkill大幅优于强非进化和自进化基线。在Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct上，F1、BLEU-1和LLM-judge分数整体平均提升31.3%；在GPT-5.4-nano上提升28.1%。

Conclusion: 将检索机制作为可进化组件来对待，通过技能表示和动态路由相结合，能够有效提升LLM代理在异构记忆查询上的表现，验证了检索中心化自进化框架的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on long-term memory for persistent interactions, the retrieval mechanisms governing this memory are rarely treated as evolvable components. This static approach limits performance on heterogeneous memory queries, which often demand diverse evidence construction strategies. To address this, we introduce \textbf{ERSkill}, a retrieval-centric framework for self-evolving, skill-guided memory access. ERSkill compiles interaction histories into a structured memory store and represents retrieval behaviors as executable skills composed of fundamental primitives. At inference time, a trained router dynamically matches each query to the optimal skill to construct tailored evidence for answer generation. To enable continuous improvement, ERSkill co-evolves the skill set and the router during training. It employs an experience trie to efficiently record explored retrieval paths, alongside a double-frontier mechanism that safely decouples the expansion of new skill capabilities from stable, router-facing deployment. Experiments across multiple agent memory benchmarks demonstrate that ERSkill substantially outperforms strong non-evolving and self-evolving baselines. Notably, it improves the overall average across F1, BLEU-1, and LLM-judge scores by 31.3\% with Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B-Instruct and by 28.1\% with GPT-5.4-nano.

</details>


### [35] [PatientAct: Theory-Grounded Mental Health Client Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12750)
*Sahand Sabour,TszYam NG,Yaqian Chen,Guanqun Bi,Jialu Zhao,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PatientAct是一个基于临床理论的客户端模拟框架，通过整合5Ps临床案例公式化与动态记忆层（带有信任阈值）来解决现有LLM模拟客户端过于合作、过早披露、缺乏抵抗行为等问题，并在40个临床情境上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM模拟客户端存在三个关键缺陷：过度合作（过早披露敏感信息）、对治疗性重构不加抵抗地接受、以及在单次会话内解决核心问题。作者将这些缺陷归因于缺乏因果深度的用户档案设计和将所有内容视为同等可访问的行为机制。

Method: PatientAct框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于5Ps临床案例公式化（呈现/诱发因素、易感因素、维持因素、保护因素）构建的用户档案，提供因果深度且不绑定特定治疗范式；(2) 动态记忆层，每个记忆项带有信任阈值（如症状早期可获得，而成形记忆需要持续的治疗联盟），模拟真实治疗中信息的逐步披露。在每一轮对话中，先模拟客户的情绪反应和行为，再生成回复。当治疗师触及被门槛保护的内容时，模型会从数量、内容、风格三个维度表达抵抗，而非默认合作或单一抵抗模式。

Result: 在40个临床情境上评估，PatientAct生成多样化的用户档案且具有高度的临床合理性。相比基线模型，PatientAct在抵抗质量和行为真实性方面取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: PatientAct通过引入临床理论指导的因果深度档案与基于信任阈值的动态记忆机制，有效改善了LLM模拟客户端的临床真实性和行为表现，为咨询师培训和LLM治疗师评估提供了更可靠的模拟环境。

Abstract: LLM-based simulated clients are increasingly used to train novice counselors, evaluate LLM therapists, and generate synthetic data. However, current simulators produce overly cooperative clients that disclose too readily, accept therapeutic reframes without resistance, and resolve core issues within a single session. We trace these issues to profiles that lack causal depth and behavioral mechanisms that treat all content as equally accessible. We present PatientAct, a framework for client simulation grounded in established clinical theories. Our profiles integrate the 5Ps clinical case formulation, providing causal depth without tying the design to any single therapeutic modality. During simulation, profiles include a dynamic memory layer in which items carry trust thresholds (e.g., symptoms are available early, whereas formative memories require a sustained therapeutic alliance). At each turn, the client's emotional reaction and behavior are modeled before generating a response. If the therapist approaches gated content, PatientAct expresses resistance in terms of quantity, content, and style rather than defaulting to cooperation or a single resistance pattern.
